# Standalone red-team pass — #1203

**Status: INCOMPLETE_BUDGET.** One owner-mandated LEAN pass only; no /feature pipeline, no second round, no counter reset. No P0/P1 was confirmed. The 14 measured candidates each killed one meaningful mutant after a green individual baseline. This is not a CLEAR result: all other current candidates remain explicitly unmeasured.

| Severity | Open | Evidence |
|---|---:|---|
| P0 | 0 | No confirmed survivor or introduced correctness defect |
| P1 | 0 | No identified uncovered production hunk, unreplaced lost behavioral coverage, fragile assertion or added broken alert path |
| P2 | 1 | RT1203-P2-01 — stale wrapper explanation at `src/app/api/admin/channels/__tests__/bindings.test.ts:95` |

ADR 0026 retains the origin allowlist and self-origin allowance. Standalone risk defaults to high; no intake exists.

## Inventory and budget

- 1549 rows: 14 killed, 1531 skipped-budget, 4 excluded retired-wrapper tests. 1545 current test instances; parameterized cases with identical titles retain distinct row IDs and collection order.
- Before measurements, static import closure identified 102 test files and 1,320 literal test definitions, with at least one direct covering test for each of 31 changed production/code files. The first supervised collection expanded this to 1,537 test instances from 101 files; it omitted one disabled live-DB file. Its single case is included from its AST declaration and remains skipped-budget; no inability-to-run baseline or live-DB verification is claimed.
- Reconciliation found one additional filesystem/golden reader after the paired measurements: `scripts/ci/__tests__/admin-contract-audit.test.ts`. Its seven instances were collected with the final launch and are all skipped-budget. Thus the initial inventory was incomplete for this indirect reader; the final inventory below includes it rather than silently sampling it out.
- Direction A includes every changed test file and all transitive reverse importers of the changed `admin-gate-mocks` helper; whole files are conservatively retained, including unchanged cases. Direction B includes direct importers and reverse transitive callers of every changed production/code file. Generated JSON schema golden readers are included separately. No changed Playwright/E2E specification or migration was identified.
- Exactly 30 supervised launches: 2 collection-only launches and 14 × (baseline + mutant). Each measurement used the exact escaped full title and passed assert-one-test. Each launch timeout was 120,000 ms; none timed out. One semantic mutation per measured test. The collection launches deliberately count against the cap even though they execute no test body. No budget continuation or another round was requested.

## Mutation evidence

| ID | Candidate | Mutation | Outcome |
|---|---|---|---|
| M01 | resolveAdminApiIdentity > refuses a cookie-only request to /api/admin/x without constructing either SDK client | Re-enable cookie authentication for ordinary Admin API requests. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |
| M02 | resolveAdminApiIdentity > preserves the existing cookie path at /api/admin/impersonation/start | Remove the permanent cookie exception. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |
| M03 | resolveAdminApiIdentity > refuses a cookie-only request to /api/admin/impersonationevil without constructing either SDK client | Admit lookalike prefixes into the cookie exception. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |
| M04 | resolveAdminApiIdentity > does not fall back inside impersonation with Authorization Basic abc | Allow a malformed bearer to fall back to the impersonation cookie. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |
| M05 | resolveAdminApiIdentity > preserves the existing cookie path at /API/ADMIN/IMPERSONATION/END/ | Drop path normalization at the canonical seam. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |
| M06 | ordinary Admin API rejects ambient cookies > requireAdminJson refuses the cookie before parsing a mutation body | Restore ordinary cookie access through requireAdminJson. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |
| M07 | ordinary Admin API rejects ambient cookies > resolveAdminActor refuses the cookie before reading its body or tenant | Restore ordinary cookie access through the channel actor gate. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |
| M08 | requireAdminWithAal2 — bearer and permanent impersonation > bearer ok | Reject valid bearer identity at the canonical seam. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |
| M09 | requireAdminWithAal2 — role and assurance refusals > bearer aal1 | Bypass strict AAL2 refusal on the surviving transport. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |
| M10 | Admin API bearer transport > refuses malformed Authorization before route work without consulting cookies | Pass malformed credentials to routes before feature/config checks. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |
| M11 | Admin API bearer transport > refuses absent or malformed credentials before /api/admin/channels/whatsapp/start missing-env checks | Remove credential refusal ordering for missing-environment channel entry. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |
| M12 | Admin API bearer transport > refuses cookie-only /api/admin/impersonation-extra without refreshing cookies | Route a cookie lookalike through the cookie proxy. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |
| M13 | Admin API bearer transport > keeps impersonation on the cookie path | Apply the ordinary bearer pass-through to permanent impersonation. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |
| M14 | POST /api/admin/businesses — channel enrollment insert > refuses cookie-only authentication even when NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL is missing | Restore the retired same-origin wrapper on an ordinary mutation. | Killed; baseline 0, mutant 1; both exactly one test |

All failures were inspected: M01/M03/M04 returned a cookie identity instead of null; M02/M05 lost the required cookie identity; M06/M07 parsed far enough to return 400 instead of 401; M08 rejected the valid bearer; M09 accepted AAL1; M10/M11 passed through with 200 instead of 401; M12 lost the ordinary refusal cache policy on a lookalike; M13 sent impersonation through with 200; M14 restored the wrong 403 wrapper refusal. No import/setup/compiler failure was counted as a kill. There were no survivors requiring arbiter confirmation.

## Fragile assertions and removed tests

Scanned added assertions for absence-of-tag proxies, rendered-string counts, order assertions and implementation-only checks. No new fragile assertion was confirmed. Scanner result counts and canonical-path assertions are the tested scanner contract; HTTP status/body/cache assertions test the refusal contract; not-called assertions test auth-before-side-effect ordering. The four removed temporary-wrapper cases and their replacement/retirement evidence have explicit excluded rows below. Renamed synthetic page fixtures preserve the prior scanner behaviors; no extra lost-coverage finding is inferred merely from their new names.

## Documentation versus HEAD

All five changed Markdown files were passed through the available prevalidate-plan.py. The four current-context/evidence files passed. The historical architecture log reported seven pre-existing issues in unchanged history (CONTEXT.md:75, removed BusinessDetailClient/carousel/ProblemSection/pricing references and two unresolved historical SHAs); the newly appended #1203 entry adds none of those citations. They are out of this diff’s scope. Current changed claims were hand-checked against the pinned implementation. The staging evidence document is explicitly PENDING and makes no completed staging or Computer Use claim.

The thirteen changed schema artifacts differ only in x-repworth-source. Every source path/line resolves to the named schema declaration at HEAD and every schema shape is byte-equivalent after removing that metadata field. The live directory preserves the five surviving Admin pages; the issue’s stale-path sweep has no remaining retired /admin page reference in the named files. /api/admin/overview and /api/admin/businesses remain valid API paths and were not mistaken for retired page paths.

- `resolveCookieIdentity|cookieBackedServerClient|requireRole\(` across Admin routes, the identity/canonical gates and `src/app/api/_shared/channels/resolve-actor.ts`: the sole production cookie resolver delegation remains inside the segment-bounded exception. Test mocks were excluded as authentication implementations.
- `/admin/(overview|businesses|flagged|jobs)` across the issue’s named fixture files and `docs/directory/app-admin.md`: surviving API paths only; those are excluded from stale-page findings.
- Added diff lines containing `catch|captureException|captureMessage|reportCaughtError|console.(warn|error)|internal_error|500`: no new operator-actionable terminal or swallowed failure path. Existing internal-error behavior is retained; therefore no new alert identity/filter/level/grouping/cadence/destination pairing requires a rule row. No staging alert-delivery drill was performed by this subagent.

## Environment, restoration and claim limits

Initial HEAD/worktree and watcher checks passed. A fresh round-0 empty journal was opened once to round 1. Every run used the same runner allowlist with temporary HOME/TMPDIR, CI/test mode, MOCK_EXTERNAL_APIS=true, no provider credentials and the live-DB disable switch. The runner atomically replaced the sole .env.local with a scrubbed copy; its original contents were never read into this report, printed or placed in git. No local database was requested; no remote DATABASE_POOL_URL was used.

Each mechanical mutation was journaled through mutation-runner mutate; restoration required exact equality with the expected mutant before writing the saved original bytes. After the last measurement all tracked paths were clean and all supervisors were inactive. The INVOKING ROOT then ran finalize: 30 empty process groups, zero remaining production files to restore, one dotenv file restored, journal closed. This report was written only after root confirmation, with a second local closed-journal/clean-tree assertion. Only findings.md and findings.json were written under the repository. No fix, commit, push, merge or deployment was performed.

Claimed gate evidence was re-read: full-final records 15,454 passed / 11 skipped; refusal-order 166 passed; final-route-shapes 535 passed; build-reviewed completed; push-second reports normal audits and test slices passing. The fallow JSON reports zero introduced issues but is pinned to older head d8658ac7b, so it is treated only as pre-fix evidence. These historical logs do not replace the 14 freshly rederived baselines. Root owns deployed staging/browser verification; no staging or Computer Use assertion is made by this pass.

## Findings

**RT1203-P2-01 — Channel binding harness still describes the removed cookie wrapper.** Open, duty 4. `src/app/api/admin/channels/__tests__/bindings.test.ts:95` says all six routes are wrapped in withSameOrigin and require NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL for that wrapper. The changed six route exports are direct bearer-gated handlers. This affects the maintenance explanation only. Update the comment when next maintaining the harness; no runtime or assertion defect was found.
